If it's a static scoreboard with a robotic voice reading numbers slowly, they bounce. PNG Add a clear on-screen visual overlay: "Subscribe for ball-by-ball updates & live fan chat", or drop an automated bot message / pin in chat reminding viewers to subscribe to participate in the live polls. i do live from rda server does it matter or prism mastter or obs matter To YouTube’s ranking algorithm, the software or server you use does not directly matter at all. YouTube does not care whether your RTMP video stream originates from an RDP/RDA server, a local PC running OBS Studio, or PRISM Live Studio. The algorithm only evaluates the incoming stream quality and viewer behavior: Stream Health & Stability: Is the stream running at a stable bitrate, 1080p60 (or 720p), without dropped frames or stuttering? Viewer Signals: Are viewers clicking (CTR), staying (Watch Time / AVD), chatting, and subscribing? However, the choice between an RDA server, OBS, and PRISM can indirectly hurt your channel depending on how you configure and run them: How Each Platform Actually Impacts Your Stream Factor OBS Studio (Local PC) PRISM Live Studio RDA / RDP Virtual Server Algorithm Penalty? None None None directly Stream Stability Dependent on home internet Dependent on home internet Extremely stable (1 Gbps+ datacenter internet) Audio/Voice Sync Perfect real-time sync Perfect real-time sync High risk of audio latency/choppiness over remote desktop Interaction & Chat Fast, responsive Built-in mobile/chat tools Clunky remote latency makes live fan interaction slower Visual Quality Native GPU encoding (NVENC) Native GPU encoding Often uses basic CPU encoding; can look blurry during fast score changes if underpowered Where Using an RDA Server Might Be Hurting You If you are streaming from an RDA/RDP server, look out for these three technical issues that frequently push viewers away: Audio Desync / Robotic Audio Clipping: Remote audio forwarded over RDP/RDA can experience slight micro-stutters, lag, or robotic distortion. If your automated Hindi commentary (Madhur AI) sounds choppy, delayed, or out of sync with the visual scoreboard updates, viewers bounce within 10 seconds. Datacenter IP Location / Regional Geo-Targeting: YouTube usually serves live content based on the target audience's language, title, and metadata, but streaming from an overseas cloud server (e.g., US, Germany, Singapore) occasionally misclassifies initial stream routing if the channel language metadata isn't strictly set to Hindi in YouTube Studio. Encoding Quality & Bitrate: Virtual RDP servers usually lack dedicated NVIDIA/AMD GPUs and rely on virtualized CPU cores. If the CPU hits 90–100% usage, the stream drops frames or suffers from pixelation, making score text hard to read on mobile screens. OBS vs. PRISM Live Studio OBS Studio: The industry benchmark. It offers total control over browser source rendering (clean transparent HTML/CSS overlays, smooth transitions, Termux/script local hooks, and audio filters like limiters/compressors). PRISM Live Studio: Built on top of the OBS engine, but adds built-in visual stickers, live chat overlays, and multi-streaming. It does not provide any algorithmic boost over standard OBS. Practical Recommendation If your home internet or PC cannot handle 4–8 hour live broadcasts, keep using the RDA server—datacenter stability is great for avoiding stream disconnects. Open your live stream on a secondary phone using mobile data and check two things: Does the commentary sound crisp and perfectly in sync with the scoreboard? Does the scoreboard look sharp on mobile without fuzzy compression artifacts? If the stream is visually clean and sound is smooth, do not worry about the software/server. Focus your energy on viewer retention: dynamic titles, readable mobile scorecards, and interactive chat polls. how to correct player names in pure hindi other commentary in hinglish .. like yadav .. its sounds yaaadav To fix this in TTS engines like Madhur, you need to intercept and replace player names before the text is sent to the speech synthesizer. When an Indian Hindi TTS engine reads English text like "Yadav", it encounters "Ya" followed by a single consonant and applies an English vowel stretch, pronouncing it as "Yaa-dav" (याऽदव). The solution is a two-step phonetic pipeline: keep your commentary in Hinglish, but pass player names and cricket terms through a Phonetic Pronunciation Dictionary (in Devanagari or phonetic spelling) before calling the TTS API. 1. Why Devanagari Works Best Madhur and similar neural engines (like ElevenLabs Multilingual, Google Cloud TTS, or Azure Hindi) natively pronounce Devanagari script with exact short/long matra precision: English: "Yadav" → Engines stretch the "a" (yaa-dav). Devanagari: यादव → The engine applies the short अ sound to द and व, pronouncing it naturally. If your script sends: "Aur yahan Yadav ne behtareen ball daali..." You transform it before sending to TTS: "Aur yahan यादव ne behtareen ball daali..." Hindi TTS engines handle mixed Devanagari + Roman script (Hinglish) without issue, but they pronounce the Devanagari words with native Hindi accents. 2. Python Replacement Dictionary If you are using a Python automation script to generate the speech, add a dictionary mapping and a regex/word-boundary replacement function: Python import re # Player Name & Cricket Term Phonetic Map PRONUNCIATION_MAP = { # Names that get stretched or mispronounced r"\bYadav\b": "यादव", r"\bSurya\b": "सूर्या", r"\bSuryakumar\b": "सूर्यकुमार", r"\bRohit\b": "रोहित", r"\bKohli\b": "कोहली", r"\bHardik\b": "हार्दिक", r"\bShreyas\b": "श्रेयस", r"\bIyer\b": "अय्यर", r"\bBumrah\b": "बुमराह", r"\bKuldeep\b": "कुलदीप", r"\bSiraj\b": "सिराज", r"\bArshdeep\b": "अर्शदीप", r"\bShubman\b": "शुभमन", r"\bGill\b": "गिल", r"\bAxar\b": "अक्षर", r"\bPant\b": "पंत", r"\bKishan\b": "किशन", r"\bPooran\b": "पूरन", r"\bHetmyer\b": "हेटमायर", r"\bRussell\b": "रसेल", r"\bHope\b": "होप", # Cricket terms that sound robotic in Hinglish r"\bOver\b": "ओवर", r"\bWicket\b": "विकेट", r"\bChowka\b": "चौका", r"\bChakka\b": "छक्का", } def fix_pronunciation(text: str) -> str: for pattern, correct_sound in PRONUNCIATION_MAP.items(): text = re.sub(pattern, correct_sound, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return text # Example usage: raw_commentary = ( "Next over lekar aaye hain Yadav, samne hain Hope aur strike par Pooran." ) clean_commentary = fix_pronunciation(raw_commentary) print(clean_commentary) # Output: "Next ओवर lekar aaye hain यादव, samne hain होप aur strike par पूरन." 3. If You Must Keep It in English Letters (Phonetic Romanization) If your specific TTS engine stumbles when mixing Hindi and English scripts in the same sentence, use phonetic Roman spelling instead of formal English spelling: Instead of "Yadav", write: "Yuduv" or "Yadv" (this forces the engine to clip the first vowel). Instead of "Iyer", write: "Ayyar". Instead of "Shreyas", write: "Shreyus". Instead of "Bumrah", write: "Boom-rah". 4. Adjusting Speech Rate for Natural Pace If the AI voice still lingers too long on syllables, check your API payload parameters: Set speaking_rate / speed to 1.05x – 1.1x. Live cricket commentary moves quickly; pushing the speed slightly above 1.0x automatically clips trailing vowels and removes the slow, robotic cadence. use phonetic Roman spelling instead of formal English spelling... get whole team players name before the match from url To ensure your commentary stays in Hinglish (Roman English) while fixing pronunciation for the Madhur AI voice, you can implement an automated pre-match script that: Fetches the entire Playing XI / Squad directly from the match URL (ESPNcricinfo or CREX / Cricbuzz) before ball one. Converts the roster into a Roman Phonetic Lookup Table so names are clipped cleanly without stretched English vowels (e.g., Yuduv instead of Yadav, Ayyur instead of Iyer). Applies Regex replacements to any incoming commentary text so the AI speaks names naturally. Complete Python Automation Script This script fetches the match page, pulls both teams' lineups, builds the phonetic Roman dictionary, and applies it to your live commentary feed. Python import json import re import requests from bs4 import BeautifulSoup # ------------------------------------------------------------- # 1. Phonetic Rules to convert formal English names to Hinglish # ------------------------------------------------------------- # Rules that prevent TTS from stretching Hindi sounds: # "Yadav" -> "Yuduv" (short 'u' sound prevents 'Yaa-dav') # "Iyer" -> "Ayyur" # "Pant" -> "Punt" # "Gill" -> "Geell" or "Gill" # "Chahal" -> "Chuhal" # ------------------------------------------------------------- COMMON_PHONETIC_CORRECTIONS = { "Yadav": "Yuduv", "Surya": "Soorya", "Suryakumar": "Sooryakumaar", "Iyer": "Ayyur", "Pant": "Punt", "Bumrah": "Boomrah", "Arshdeep": "Urshdeep", "Chahal": "Chuhal", "Kuldeep": "Kooldeep", "Hardik": "Haardik", "Shubman": "Shoobman", "Ruturaj": "Rootooraaj", "Pooran": "Pooran", "Hetmyer": "Hetmaayur", "Russell": "Russul", "Shepherd": "Shephurd", "Holder": "Holdur", } def create_phonetic_name(formal_name: str) -> str: """Takes a player's formal English name and applies Roman phonetic rules so the Madhur AI voice pronounces it crisply. """ parts = formal_name.strip().split() phonetic_parts = [] for part in parts: clean_word = re.sub(r"[^\w]", "", part) if clean_word in COMMON_PHONETIC_CORRECTIONS: phonetic_parts.append(COMMON_PHONETIC_CORRECTIONS[clean_word]) else: # General phonetics: short vowels for Indian surnames ending in 'av' or 'ar' if clean_word.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv converted = clean_word[:-2] + "uv" elif clean_word.endswith("an") and len(clean_word) > 4: # e.g., Ishan converted = clean_word else: converted = clean_word phonetic_parts.append(converted) return " ".join(phonetic_parts) # ------------------------------------------------------------- # 2. Extract Squads from Match URL (ESPNcricinfo Example) # ------------------------------------------------------------- def get_squad_from_url(match_url: str) -> dict: """Fetches the webpage and extracts player names. Returns a dict mapping: { 'Formal Name': 'Phonetic Roman Name' } """ headers = { "User-Agent": ( "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML," " like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36" ) } print(f"[i] Fetching lineup from: {match_url}") response = requests.get(match_url, headers=headers) soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, "html.parser") player_dict = {} # Method A: Parse next.js / embedded JSON state (standard on ESPNcricinfo & CREX) next_data = soup.find("script", id="__NEXT_DATA__") if next_data: try: data = json.loads(next_data.string) # ESPNcricinfo stores match players under match details or scorecard players match_players = [] # Traverse JSON to find player names automatically serialized = json.dumps(data) names = re.findall(r'"longName":"([^"]+)"', serialized) or re.findall( r'"name":"([^"]+)"', serialized ) for name in set(names): # Filter out umpire/stadium names by checking reasonable name lengths if 3 < len(name) < 25 and not any( x in name.lower() for x in ["stadium", "series", "trophy", "cup"] ): player_dict[name] = create_phonetic_name(name) # Also map just the surname (e.g. 'Yadav') surname = name.split()[-1] if len(surname) > 3: player_dict[surname] = create_phonetic_name(surname) except Exception as e: print(f"[!] JSON parsing fallback: {e}") # Method B: Fallback HTML scraping for player link anchors if not player_dict: for a in soup.find_all("a", href=True): if "/cricketers/" in a["href"] or "/player/" in a["href"]: name = a.text.strip() if name and len(name) > 3: player_dict[name] = create_phonetic_name(name) surname = name.split()[-1] player_dict[surname] = create_phonetic_name(surname) return player_dict # ------------------------------------------------------------- # 3. Commentary Transformer Engine # ------------------------------------------------------------- class CommentaryProcessor: def __init__(self, phonetic_map: dict): self.phonetic_map = phonetic_map # Sort keys by length in descending order so full names replace before surnames sorted_names = sorted( self.phonetic_map.keys(), key=lambda x: len(x), reverse=True ) # Compile regex pattern with word boundaries self.regex = re.compile( r"\b(" + "|".join(re.escape(name) for name in sorted_names if name.strip()) + r")\b", re.IGNORECASE, ) def transform(self, commentary_text: str) -> str: """Replaces formal names with Roman phonetic counterparts.""" def replacer(match): word = match.group(0) # Case-insensitive match in dictionary for original, phonetic in self.phonetic_map.items(): if original.lower() == word.lower(): return phonetic return word return self.regex.sub(replacer, commentary_text) # ============================================================= # Practical Execution Workflow Before Match Starts # ============================================================= if __name__ == "__main__": # Put your ESPNcricinfo or CREX match scorecard / preview URL here SAMPLE_URL = ( "https://www.espncricinfo.com/series/west-indies-tour-of-india-2026-..." ) # 1. Fetch team players once at toss / before 1st ball: # (Simulated map for instant testing) active_lineup = { "Suryakumar Yadav": "Sooryakumaar Yuduv", "Yadav": "Yuduv", "Shreyas Iyer": "Shreyus Ayyur", "Iyer": "Ayyur", "Rishabh Pant": "Rishubh Punt", "Pant": "Punt", "Shai Hope": "Shai Hope", "Nicholas Pooran": "Nikolus Pooran", "Arshdeep Singh": "Urshdeep Singh", } processor = CommentaryProcessor(active_lineup) # 2. During the live match, pass CREX/Hinglish commentary text through processor: incoming_commentary = [ "Yadav ne outswing ball daali aur Pant ne brilliant catch liya!", "Iyer abhi strike par hain aur Yadav bowling end par tayyar.", "Suryakumar Yadav ne fine leg par ek shandar chowka lagaya.", ] print("\n--- Testing Hinglish TTS Pronunciation Output ---\n") for line in incoming_commentary: processed_line = processor.transform(line) print(f"Original : {line}") print(f"To Madhur: {processed_line}\n") Output Example sent to Madhur: Original: "Yadav ne outswing ball daali aur Pant ne brilliant catch liya!" Transformed: "Yuduv ne outswing ball daali aur Punt ne brilliant catch liya!" (Result: The AI says "यदुव / यादव" swiftly instead of lingering on "याऽदव"). Original: "Iyer abhi strike par hain aur Yadav bowling end par tayyar." Transformed: "Ayyur abhi strike par hain aur Yuduv bowling end par tayyar." Exception: return "अगली गेंद" # ============================================================ # MASTER PHONETIC DICTIONARY # ============================================================ COMMON_NAMES = "ध"), ("jh", "झ"), ("ee", "ी"), ("oo", "ू"), ("aa", "ा"), ("ai", "ै"), ("au", "ौ"), ("a", "ा"), ("b", "ब"), ("c", "क"), ("d", "ड"), ("e", "े"), ("f", "फ"), ("g", "ग"), ("h", "ह"), ("i", "ि"), ("j", ) def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub(r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() result = [] for word in cleaned.split(): lower = word.lower() if lower in COMMON_NAMES: result.append(COMMON_NAMES[lower]) else: word_hi, idx, length = "", 0, len(lower) while idx < length: matched = False for pattern, repl in PHONIC_RULES: if lower.startswith(pattern, idx): word_hi += repl idx += len(pattern) matched = True break if not matched: idx += 1 result.append(word_hi if word_hi else word) return " ".join(result) # ============================================================ # NATURAL RADIO COMMENTARY text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = int(str(runs).strip()) wkts_num = int(str(wkts).strip()) except ============================================================ def get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = get_short_path(long_name, output_buf, output_buf_size) return output_buf.value if r > 0 else long_name def play_mp3_native_windows(mp3_path: str): winmm = ctypes.windll.winmm alias = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW('close all', None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type mpegvideo alias {alias}' ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(open_cmd, None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(f'open {safe_path} alias {alias}', None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: return winmm.mciSendStringW(f'play {alias} wait', None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f'close {alias}', None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text).replace("\r", " ").replace("\n", " ").strip() if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+15%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+8%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+3%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3") try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate(clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r'''() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\vert{}20\.0\vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }''') if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal") ) except Exception: return None # ============================================================ # NETWORK RESOURCE FILTER # ============================================================ async def route_filter(route: Route) -> None: req = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await route.abort() return await route.continue_() # ============================================================ # MAIN COMMENTARY LOOP # ============================================================ def print_status(message: str) -> None: print(f"\r{message:<70}", end="", flush=True) async def open_match_page(page: Page, target_url: str) -> None: try: await page.goto(target_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS) await page.wait_for_timeout(3000) except Exception as exc: print("Page load notice:", exc) async def announce_over_end(page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, ball_num: str) -> None: await asyncio.sleep(OVER_SETTLE_SECONDS) fresh = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) runs = fresh.runs if fresh else None wkts = fresh.wkts if fresh else None over_int = int(ball_num.split(".")[0]) + 1 print(f"\n📊 Extracted Active Score: {runs}/{wkts} after {over_int} overs") score_msg = build_over_summary(over_int, runs, wkts) print("📢 OVER ANNOUNCEMENT:", score_msg) await announcer.say(score_msg, 2) async def handle_new_ball(page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, snap: BallSnapshot) -> None: assert snap.ball and snap.circle_val print("\n" + "=" * 65) print(f"🏏 CIRCLE BALL: {snap.ball} | BADGE: [{snap.circle_val}]") dismissal_data = snap.last_dismissal if snap.circle_val.lower() in ("w", "wkt", "out"): await asyncio.sleep(WICKET_CARD_WAIT_SECONDS) fresh_snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if fresh_snap and fresh_snap.last_dismissal: dismissal_data = fresh_snap.last_dismissal if dismissal_data: print(f"🎯 WICKET DETAILS: {dismissal_data}") print("=" * 65) text, rate_level = generate_circle_ball_commentary( snap.ball, snap.circle_val, snap.bowler, snap.batsman, dismissal_data ) print("🇮🇳 RADIO HINGLISH:", text) await announcer.say(text, rate_level) if snap.is_over_complete: await announce_over_end(page, announcer, snap.ball) async def monitor_loop(page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer) -> None: last_ball_seen: Optional[str] = None failures = 0 while True: try: snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if snap and snap.has_ball: if snap.ball != last_ball_seen: last_ball_seen = snap.ball await handle_new_ball(page, announcer, snap) else: print_status(f"⏳ Monitoring... Last Circle Ball: {last_ball_seen} [{snap.circle_val}]") else: print_status("⏳ Waiting for circle balls strip to update...") failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(POLL_SECONDS) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception as exc: failures += 1 log.warning("Loop error (%d in a row): %s", failures, exc) if failures >= RELOAD_AFTER_FAILURES: try: await page.reload(wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS) except Exception: pass failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(3) async def run_live_stream(target_url: str) -> None: print("=" * 65) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - PROFESSIONAL CREX RADIO ENGINE") print("🎙 VOICE: Microsoft Madhur Neural (Accurate Wicket Engine)") print("=" * 65) print(f"🎯 TARGET URL: {target_url}\n") print("🚀 Initializing browser...") announcer = RadioAnnouncer() announcer.start() try: async with async_playwright() as p: browser = await p.chromium.launch( headless=True, args=[ "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled", "--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage", "--disable-gpu", ], ) try: context = await browser.new_context( viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 800}, user_agent=USER_AGENT, ) page = await context.new_page() await page.route("**/*", route_filter) print("🌐 Connecting to CREX Match URL...") await open_match_page(page, target_url) print("✅ CONNECTED TO CREX") print("🔴 LIVE COMMENTARY READY (Press CTRL+C to exit)\n") await monitor_loop(page, announcer) finally: await browser.close() finally: await announcer.stop() def main() -> None: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S") configure_stdio() disable_quickedit() prevent_sleep_and_throttle() try: print("\n" + "=" * 60) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - CREX RADIO RUNNER") print("=" * 60) user_url = input("Enter CREX Match URL (Press ENTER to use default):\n> ").strip() final_url = user_url or DEFAULT_URL asyncio.run(run_live_stream(final_url)) except KeyboardInterrupt: print("\n\n🛑 Script stopped by user.") except Exception: traceback.print_exc() input("\nPress ENTER to close...") if __name__ == "__main__": main() Here is the complete, production-ready script. Key Enhancements Added Dynamic Squad Extraction from CREX: When the match page loads, the script scrapes both teams' full Playing XI and squads right from the DOM/Scorecard tabs into a memory cache. True Indian Phonetic Engine: Replaced the character-by-character replacement with a syllable-aware phonetic pipeline. Indian names with short vowels like Yadav, Iyer, Pant, Shreyas, Bumrah, and Chahal are transformed to clean Devanagari/Hindi equivalents (e.g., यादव, अय्यर, पंत), preventing Microsoft Madhur from dragging or mispronouncing syllables like "Yaa-dav". Foreign Player Dictionary: Pre-mapped common international names (Russell, Hope, Pooran, Hetmyer, Shepherd, Hosein, etc.) to natural Hindi phonetics. Clean Fallback: If an unlisted name is encountered, it applies Indian syllable clipping rules so the voice maintains natural cadence. "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= "मैथ्यू", # Emirates D10 / Associate Players "muditha": "मुदिथा", "disanayaka": "डिसानायका", "aftab": "आफताब", "alam": "आलम", "shah": "शाह", "aryansh": "आर्यांश", "sharma": "शर्मा", "rai": "राय", "ethan": "ईथन", "carl": "कार्ल", "dsouza": "डिसूज़ा", "vicky": "विक्की", "ranjan": "रंजन", "wahab": "वहाब", "hassan": "हसन", "zadran": "जादरान", "panoly": "पनोली", "sabnani": "सबनानी", "parashar": "पाराशर", "asif": "आसिफ", "taniwal": "तानीवाल", "noorullah": "नूरुल्लाह", "ayobi": "अय्यूबी", } def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: """Converts English player names into natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page: Page) -> None: """Extracts all player names from the page and pre-caches their pronunciation.""" try: names = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { const collected = new dismissal_text: str, batter_name: str, batter_runs: str, batter_balls: str ) -> str: batter_hi = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batter_name) runs_val = int(batter_runs) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() else 0 runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_val) balls_hi = ( number_to_hindi_words(batter_balls) if str(batter_balls).isdigit() else "" ) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val > 0: score_status = ( ओर रुख करते हुए" d_clean = (dismissal_text or "").lower() cb_match = re.search(r"c\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+b\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if cb_match: fielder = {batter_hi} {score_status}" " वापस लौटते हुए!" ) if "run out" in d_clean: return ( f"खराब तालमेल! {batter_hi} {score_status} रन आउट होकर पवेलियन लौटते" " हुए!" ) return ( f"और ये बहुत बड़ा झटका! f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ फैलाए, वाइड बॉल!", 2, ) if badge in ("nb", "no से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def Array.from(collected); }""") count = 0 for name in names: for part in name.split(): p_clean = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", part).lower() if p_clean and len(p_clean) > 2 and p_clean not in dismissal_text: str, batter_name: str, batter_runs: str, batter_balls: str ) -> str: batter_hi = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batter_name) runs_val = int(batter_runs) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() else 0 runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_val) balls_hi = ( number_to_hindi_words(batter_balls) if str(batter_balls).isdigit() else "" ) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val > 0: score_status = ( f"{balls_hi} गेंदों में {runs_hi} रन बनाकर" if balls_hi else f"{runs_hi} रन बनाकर" ) elif str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val == 0: score_status = "बिना खाता खोले" else: score_status = "पवेलियन की ओर रुख करते हुए" d_clean = (dismissal_text or "").lower() cb_match = re.search(r"c\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+b\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if cb_match: fielder = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(1).strip()) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(2).strip()) return ( f"विकेट पतन! बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status}, {bowler} की गेंद" f" पर {fielder} को आसान कैच थमा बैठे!" ) b_match = re.search(r"\bb\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if b_match and "c " not in d_clean: bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(b_match.group(1).strip()) return ( {batter_hi} {score_status}" " वापस लौटते हुए!" ) if "run out" in d_clean: return ( f"खराब तालमेल! {batter_hi} {score_status} रन आउट होकर पवेलियन लौटते" " हुए!" ) return ( f"और ये बहुत बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status} आउट होकर पवेलियन" " लौटते हुए!" ) def generate_circle_ball_commentary( ball_num: str, circle_val: Optional[str], bowler_raw: Optional[str], batsman_raw: Optional[str], f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ फैलाए, वाइड बॉल!", 2, ) if badge in ("nb", "no action = random.choice(HINGLISH_FOUR) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "3": action = ( "शानदार रनिंग बिटवीन द विकेट्स, दोनों बल्लेबाजों ने दौड़कर तीन रन" " पूरे किए!" ) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "2": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOUBLE) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "1": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_SINGLE) rate_level = 2 else: action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOT) rate_level = 1 runup = random.choice(BOWLER_RUNUPS) if bowler and batsman: text = ( f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} {runup} गेंद डालते हुए {batsman} को," f" {action}" ) elif bowler: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} गेंदबाजी छोर से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = int(str(runs).strip()) wkts_num = int(str(wkts).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return f"और इस तरह {over_word} ओवर का खेल समाप्त हुआ।" runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_num) wkts_hi = number_to_hindi_words(wkts_num) crr = round(runs_num / max(over_int, 1), 2) crr_hi = number_to_hindi_words(int(crr)) if crr >= 8.5: analysis = ( f"करंट रन रेट {crr_hi} के पार पहुंच चुका है, बल्लेबाज पूरी तरह हावी" " हैं।" ) elif crr >= 6.5: analysis = ( f"रन गति लगभग {crr_hi} प्रति ओवर की है, दोनों टीमें रणनीति पर कायम" " हैं।" ) else: analysis = ( f"रन गति {crr_hi} के आसपास थोड़ी धीमी जरूर है, अब बड़े शॉट्स की" " तलाश होगी।" ) return ( f"ओवर समाप्त! {over_word} ओवर के बाद कुल स्कोर {runs_hi} रन, {wkts_hi}" f" विकेट के नुकसान पर। {analysis}" ) # ============================================================ # BULLETPROOF SHORT-PATH WINDOWS MCI AUDIO ENGINE # ============================================================ def get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = get_short_path(long_name, output_buf, output_buf_size) return output_buf.value if r > 0 else long_name def play_mp3_native_windows(mp3_path: str): winmm = ctypes.windll.winmm alias = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW("close all", None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type mpegvideo alias {alias}' ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(open_cmd, None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(f"open {safe_path} alias {alias}", None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: return winmm.mciSendStringW(f"play {alias} wait", None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f"close {alias}", None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = ( re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= "ethan": "ईथन", "carl": "कार्ल", "dsouza": "डिसूज़ा", "vicky": "विक्की", "ranjan": "रंजन", "wahab": "वहाब", "hassan": "हसन", "zadran": "जादरान", "panoly": "पनोली", "sabnani": "सबनानी", "parashar": "पाराशर", "asif": "आसिफ", "taniwal": "तानीवाल", "noorullah": "नूरुल्लाह", "ayobi": "अय्यूबी", } def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: """Converts English player names into natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def Array.from(collected); }""") count = 0 for name in names: for part in name.split(): p_clean = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", part).lower() if p_clean and len(p_clean) > 2 and p_clean not in re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # ============================================================ # NETWORK RESOURCE FILTER # ============================================================ async def route_filter(route: Route) -> None: req = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await route.abort() return await route.continue_() # ============================================================ # MAIN COMMENTARY LOOP # ============================================================ def print_status(message: str) -> None: print(f"\r{message:<70}", end="", flush=True) async def open_match_page(page: Page, target_url: str) -> None: try: await page.goto( target_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS ) await page.wait_for_timeout(3000) await auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page) except Exception as exc: print("Page load notice:", exc) async def announce_over_end( page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, ball_num: str ) -> None: await asyncio.sleep(OVER_SETTLE_SECONDS) fresh = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) runs = fresh.runs if fresh else None wkts = fresh.wkts if fresh else None over_int = int(ball_num.split(".")[0]) + 1 print(f"\n📊 Extracted Active Score: {runs}/{wkts} after {over_int} overs") score_msg = build_over_summary(over_int, runs, wkts) print("📢 OVER ANNOUNCEMENT:", score_msg) await announcer.say(score_msg, 2) async def handle_new_ball( page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, snap: BallSnapshot ) -> None: assert snap.ball and snap.circle_val print("\n" + "=" * 65) print(f"🏏 CIRCLE BALL: {snap.ball} | BADGE: [{snap.circle_val}]") dismissal_data = snap.last_dismissal if snap.circle_val.lower() in ("w", "wkt", "out"): await asyncio.sleep(WICKET_CARD_WAIT_SECONDS) fresh_snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if fresh_snap and fresh_snap.last_dismissal: dismissal_data = fresh_snap.last_dismissal if dismissal_data: print(f"🎯 WICKET DETAILS: {dismissal_data}") print("=" * 65) text, rate_level = generate_circle_ball_commentary( snap.ball, snap.circle_val, snap.bowler, snap.batsman, dismissal_data ) print("🇮🇳 RADIO HINGLISH:", text) await announcer.say(text, rate_level) if snap.is_over_complete: await announce_over_end(page, announcer, snap.ball) async def monitor_loop(page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer) -> None: last_ball_seen: Optional[str] = None failures = 0 while True: try: snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if snap and snap.has_ball: if snap.ball != last_ball_seen: last_ball_seen = snap.ball await handle_new_ball(page, announcer, snap) else: print_status( f"⏳ Monitoring... Last Circle Ball: {last_ball_seen}" f" [{snap.circle_val}]" ) else: print_status("⏳ Waiting for circle balls strip to update...") failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(POLL_SECONDS) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception as exc: failures += 1 log.warning("Loop error (%d in a row): %s", failures, exc) if failures >= RELOAD_AFTER_FAILURES: try: await page.reload( wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS ) await auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page) except Exception: pass failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(3) async def run_live_stream(target_url: str) -> None: print("=" * 65) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - PROFESSIONAL CREX RADIO ENGINE") print("🎙 VOICE: Microsoft Madhur Neural (Accurate Wicket Engine)") print("=" * 65) print(f"🎯 TARGET URL: {target_url}\n") print("🚀 Initializing browser...") announcer = RadioAnnouncer() announcer.start() try: async with async_playwright() as p: browser = await p.chromium.launch( headless=True, args=[ "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled", "--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage", "--disable-gpu", ], ) try: context = await browser.new_context( viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 800}, user_agent=USER_AGENT, ) page = await context.new_page() await page.route("**/*", route_filter) print("🌐 Connecting to CREX Match URL...") await open_match_page(page, target_url) print("✅ CONNECTED TO CREX") print("🔴 LIVE COMMENTARY READY (Press CTRL+C to exit)\n") await monitor_loop(page, announcer) finally: await browser.close() finally: await announcer.stop() def main() -> None: logging.basicConfig( level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S", ) configure_stdio() disable_quickedit() prevent_sleep_and_throttle() try: print("\n" + "=" * 60) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - CREX RADIO RUNNER") print("=" * 60) user_url = input( "Enter CREX Match URL (Press ENTER to use default):\n> " ).strip() final_url = user_url or DEFAULT_URL asyncio.run(run_live_stream(final_url)) except KeyboardInterrupt: print("\n\n🛑 Script stopped by user.") except Exception: traceback.print_exc() input("\nPress ENTER to close...") if __name__ == ============================================================ # WINDOWS TUNING # ============================================================ def disable_quickedit() -> None: if not IS_WINDOWS: return try: kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32 h_stdin = kernel32.GetStdHandle(-10) mode = ctypes.c_ulong() kernel32.GetConsoleMode(h_stdin, ctypes.byref(mode)) kernel32.SetConsoleMode(h_stdin, mode.value & ~0x0040) except Exception: pass def prevent_sleep_and_throttle() -> None: if not IS_WINDOWS: return try: kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32 kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(0x80000000 | 0x00000001 | 0x00000040) kernel32.SetPriorityClass(kernel32.GetCurrentProcess(), 0x00008000) except Exception: pass def configure_stdio() -> None: for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr): try: "छियानवे", 97: "सत्तानवे", 98: "अट्ठानवे", 99: "निन्यानवे", 100: "एक सौ", } HINDI_HUNDREDS = { 1: "एक सौ", 2: "दो सौ", 3: "तीन सौ", 4: "चार सौ", 5: "पांच सौ", 6: "छह सौ", 7: "सात सौ", 8: "आठ सौ", 9: "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= 999: hundreds, rem = divmod(n, 100) head = HINDI_HUNDREDS.get(hundreds, f"{hundreds} सौ") return head if rem == 0 else f"{head} {NUM_TO_HINDI.get(rem, str(rem))}" return str(n) def over_ordinal_hindi(over_num: int) -> str: return HINDI_OVER_WORDS.get(over_num, f"{number_to_hindi_words(over_num)}वें") def format_ball_phrase_hindi(ball_str: str) -> str: try: parts = ball_str.strip().split(".") over_num = int(parts[0]) + 1 raw_ball_idx = int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else 1 ball_idx = min(max(raw_ball_idx, 1), 6) ball_name = HINDI_BALL_WORDS.get(ball_idx, f"{ball_idx}वीं गेंद") return f"{over_ordinal_hindi(over_num)} ओवर की {ball_name}" except Exception: return "अगली गेंद" # ============================================================ # DYNAMIC "मैथ्यू", # Emirates D10 / Associate Players "muditha": "मुदिथा", "disanayaka": "डिसानायका", "aftab": "आफताब", "alam": "आलम", "shah": "शाह", "aryansh": "आर्यांश", "sharma": "शर्मा", "rai": "राय", "ethan": "ईथन", "carl": "कार्ल", "dsouza": "डिसूज़ा", "vicky": "विक्की", "ranjan": "रंजन", "wahab": "वहाब", "hassan": "हसन", "zadran": "जादरान", "panoly": "पनोली", "sabnani": "सबनानी", "parashar": "पाराशर", "asif": "आसिफ", "taniwal": "तानीवाल", "noorullah": "नूरुल्लाह", "ayobi": "अय्यूबी", } def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: """Converts English player names into natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page: Page) -> None: """Extracts all player names from the page and pre-caches their pronunciation.""" try: names = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { const collected = new Set(); // Look for squad links, table cells, and player tags const nodes = document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/player/"], div[class*="player-name"], span[class*="player-name"], div[class*="batsman"]'); for (let n of nodes) { const txt = (n.innerText || '').trim(); if (txt.length > 2 && txt.length < 25 && !/\d/.test(txt)) { collected.add(txt); } } return Array.from(collected); }""") count = 0 for name in names: for part in name.split(): p_clean = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", part).lower() if p_clean and len(p_clean) > 2 and p_clean not in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: if p_clean in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[p_clean] = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[p_clean] count += 1 print( f"📋 Auto-loaded {len(names)} players from CREX DOM ({count} active" " phonetics ready)" ) except Exception as e: log.warning("Squad auto-detection skipped: %s", e) # ============================================================ # NATURAL RADIO COMMENTARY SCRIPTS # ============================================================ HINGLISH_SIX = ( ( "हवाई फायर! बल्ले के बिल्कुल बीचों-बीच कनेक्ट हुई, गेंद दर्शकों के बीच" " छह रन के लिए!" ), "स्वीट स्पॉट से पास कोई मौका नहीं, गेंद गोली की रफ्तार से" " बाउंड्री पार चार रन!" ), ( "कलाईयों का जादुई इस्तेमाल, खूबसूरत प्लेसमेंट और गेंद सीमा रेखा के" " बाहर, चौका!" ), "नज़ाकत से गैप ढूंढा और गेंद बिजली की फुर्ती से बाउंड्री लाइन पार कर गई!", ) HINGLISH_DOT = ( ( "कसी हुई लाइन और लेंथ, बल्लेबाज ने संभलकर खेला, कोई रन नहीं मिलेगा," " डॉट बॉल।" ), ( "शानदार गेंद! बल्लेबाज को पूरी तरह छकाया, गेंद सीधे
