स्ट्राइक रोटेट की, आसानी से एक रन चुरा लिया।", ) HINGLISH_DOUBLE = ( ( "गैप में खेलकर दो फील्डर्स के बीच से बढ़िया रनिंग, आसानी से दो रन पूरे" " किए!" ), "सॉफ्ट हैंड्स से धकेला, बढ़िया तालमेल और दौड़कर दो रन निकाले!", ) BOWLER_RUNUPS = ( "अंपायर को पार करते हुए", "अपने रन-अप से तेज कदमों के साथ", "क्रीज का बढ़िया इस्तेमाल करते हुए", "हल्के कदमों से दौड़ते हुए", ) def build_live_wicket_speech( dismissal_text: str, batter_name: str, batter_runs: str, batter_balls: str ) -> str: batter_hi = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batter_name) runs_val = int(batter_runs) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() else 0 runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_val) balls_hi = ( number_to_hindi_words(batter_balls) if str(batter_balls).isdigit() else "" ) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val > 0: score_status = ( f"{balls_hi} गेंदों में {runs_hi} रन बनाकर" if balls_hi else f"{runs_hi} रन बनाकर" ) elif str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val == 0: score_status = "बिना खाता खोले" else: score_status = "पवेलियन की ओर रुख करते हुए" d_clean = (dismissal_text or "").lower() cb_match = re.search(r"c\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+b\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if cb_match: fielder = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(1).strip()) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(2).strip()) return ( f"विकेट पतन! बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status}, {bowler} की गेंद" f" पर {fielder} को आसान कैच थमा बैठे!" ) b_match = re.search(r"\bb\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if b_match and "c " not in d_clean: bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(b_match.group(1).strip()) return ( f"गिल्लियां हवा में बिखर गईं! क्लीन बोल्ड! {batter_hi} {score_status}," f" {bowler} की जादुई गेंद पर बोल्ड हो गए!" ) if "lbw" in d_clean: return ( f"अंपायर की उंगली हवा में! एलबीडब्ल्यू आउट! {batter_hi} {score_status}" " वापस लौटते हुए!" ) if "run out" in d_clean: return ( f"खराब तालमेल! {batter_hi} {score_status} रन आउट होकर पवेलियन लौटते" " हुए!" ) return ( f"और ये बहुत बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status} आउट होकर पवेलियन" " लौटते हुए!" ) def generate_circle_ball_commentary( ball_num: str, circle_val: Optional[str], bowler_raw: Optional[str], batsman_raw: Optional[str], last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None, ) -> tuple[str, int]: ball_phrase = format_ball_phrase_hindi(ball_num) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(bowler_raw) batsman = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batsman_raw) badge = (circle_val or "").strip().lower() # WICKET PRIORITY if badge in ("w", "wkt", "out"): batter_to_announce = batsman_raw runs_to_announce = "" balls_to_announce = "" how_out = f"b {bowler_raw}" if bowler_raw else "" if last_dismissal and last_dismissal.get("batter"): card_b = last_dismissal.get("batter", "") if batsman_raw and any( part.lower() in card_b.lower() for part in batsman_raw.split() if len(part) > 2 ): batter_to_announce = card_b runs_to_announce = last_dismissal.get("runs", "") balls_to_announce = last_dismissal.get("balls", "") how_out = last_dismissal.get("howOut", how_out) wkt_speech = build_live_wicket_speech( how_out, batter_to_announce, runs_to_announce, balls_to_announce ) return f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ फैलाए, वाइड बॉल!", 2, ) if badge in ("nb", "no ball"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, नो बॉल! गेंदबाज ने क्रीज को ओवरस्टेप किया, अब अगली गेंद" " फ्री हिट!", 2, ) if badge == "6": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_SIX) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "4": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_FOUR) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "3": action = ( "शानदार रनिंग बिटवीन द विकेट्स, दोनों बल्लेबाजों ने दौड़कर तीन रन" " पूरे किए!" ) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "2": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOUBLE) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "1": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_SINGLE) rate_level = 2 else: action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOT) rate_level = 1 runup = random.choice(BOWLER_RUNUPS) if bowler and batsman: text = ( f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} {runup} गेंद डालते हुए {batsman} को," f" {action}" ) elif bowler: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} गेंदबाजी छोर से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = int(str(runs).strip()) wkts_num = int(str(wkts).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return f"और इस तरह {over_word} ओवर का खेल समाप्त हुआ।" runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_num) wkts_hi = number_to_hindi_words(wkts_num) crr = round(runs_num / max(over_int, 1), 2) crr_hi = number_to_hindi_words(int(crr)) if crr >= 8.5: analysis = ( f"करंट रन रेट {crr_hi} के पार पहुंच चुका है, बल्लेबाज पूरी तरह हावी" " हैं।" ) elif crr >= 6.5: analysis = ( f"रन गति लगभग {crr_hi} प्रति ओवर की है, दोनों टीमें रणनीति पर कायम" " हैं।" ) else: analysis = ( f"रन गति {crr_hi} के आसपास थोड़ी धीमी जरूर है, अब बड़े शॉट्स की" " तलाश होगी।" ) return ( f"ओवर समाप्त! {over_word} ओवर के बाद कुल स्कोर {runs_hi} रन, {wkts_hi}" f" विकेट के नुकसान पर। {analysis}" ) # ============================================================ # BULLETPROOF SHORT-PATH WINDOWS MCI AUDIO ENGINE # ============================================================ def get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = get_short_path(long_name, output_buf, output_buf_size) return output_buf.value if r > 0 else long_name def play_mp3_native_windows(mp3_path: str): winmm = ctypes.windll.winmm alias = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW("close all", None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type mpegvideo alias {alias}' ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(open_cmd, None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(f"open {safe_path} alias {alias}", None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: return winmm.mciSendStringW(f"play {alias} wait", None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f"close {alias}", None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = ( re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # ============================================================ # NETWORK RESOURCE FILTER # ============================================================ async def route_filter(route: Route) -> None: req = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await route.abort() return await route.continue_() # ============================================================ # MAIN COMMENTARY LOOP # ============================================================ def print_status(message: str) -> None: print(f"\r{message:<70}", end="", flush=True) async def open_match_page(page: Page, target_url: str) -> None: try: await page.goto( target_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS ) await page.wait_for_timeout(3000) await auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page) except Exception as exc: print("Page load notice:", exc) async def announce_over_end( page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, ball_num: str ) -> None: await asyncio.sleep(OVER_SETTLE_SECONDS) fresh = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) runs = fresh.runs if fresh else None wkts = fresh.wkts if fresh else None over_int = int(ball_num.split(".")[0]) + 1 print(f"\n📊 Extracted Active Score: {runs}/{wkts} after {over_int} overs") score_msg = build_over_summary(over_int, runs, wkts) print("📢 OVER ANNOUNCEMENT:", score_msg) await announcer.say(score_msg, 2) async def handle_new_ball( page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, snap: BallSnapshot ) -> None: assert snap.ball and snap.circle_val print("\n" + "=" * 65) print(f"🏏 CIRCLE BALL: {snap.ball} | BADGE: [{snap.circle_val}]") dismissal_data = snap.last_dismissal if snap.circle_val.lower() in ("w", "wkt", "out"): await asyncio.sleep(WICKET_CARD_WAIT_SECONDS) fresh_snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if fresh_snap and fresh_snap.last_dismissal: dismissal_data = fresh_snap.last_dismissal if dismissal_data: print(f"🎯 WICKET DETAILS: {dismissal_data}") print("=" * 65) text, rate_level = generate_circle_ball_commentary( snap.ball, snap.circle_val, snap.bowler, snap.batsman, dismissal_data ) print("🇮🇳 RADIO HINGLISH:", text) await announcer.say(text, rate_level) if snap.is_over_complete: await announce_over_end(page, announcer, snap.ball) async def monitor_loop(page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer) -> None: last_ball_seen: Optional[str] = None failures = 0 while True: try: snap = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) if snap and snap.has_ball: if snap.ball != last_ball_seen: last_ball_seen = snap.ball await handle_new_ball(page, announcer, snap) else: print_status( f"⏳ Monitoring... Last Circle Ball: {last_ball_seen}" f" [{snap.circle_val}]" ) else: print_status("⏳ Waiting for circle balls strip to update...") failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(POLL_SECONDS) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception as exc: failures += 1 log.warning("Loop error (%d in a row): %s", failures, exc) if failures >= RELOAD_AFTER_FAILURES: try: await page.reload( wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=PAGE_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS ) await auto_extract_squad_from_crex(page) except Exception: pass failures = 0 await asyncio.sleep(3) async def run_live_stream(target_url: str) -> None: print("=" * 65) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - PROFESSIONAL CREX RADIO ENGINE") print("🎙 VOICE: Microsoft Madhur Neural (Accurate Wicket Engine)") print("=" * 65) print(f"🎯 TARGET URL: {target_url}\n") print("🚀 Initializing browser...") announcer = RadioAnnouncer() announcer.start() try: async with async_playwright() as p: browser = await p.chromium.launch( headless=True, args=[ "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled", "--no-sandbox", "--disable-dev-shm-usage", "--disable-gpu", ], ) try: context = await browser.new_context( viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 800}, user_agent=USER_AGENT, ) page = await context.new_page() await page.route("**/*", route_filter) print("🌐 Connecting to CREX Match URL...") await open_match_page(page, target_url) print("✅ CONNECTED TO CREX") print("🔴 LIVE COMMENTARY READY (Press CTRL+C to exit)\n") await monitor_loop(page, announcer) finally: await browser.close() finally: await announcer.stop() def main() -> None: logging.basicConfig( level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S", ) configure_stdio() disable_quickedit() prevent_sleep_and_throttle() try: print("\n" + "=" * 60) print("🏏 PREET SPORTS - CREX RADIO RUNNER") print("=" * 60) user_url = input( "Enter CREX Match URL (Press ENTER to use default):\n> " ).strip() final_url = user_url or DEFAULT_URL ============================================================ # WINDOWS TUNING # ============================================================ def disable_quickedit() -> None: if not IS_WINDOWS: return ~0x0040) except Exception: pass def prevent_sleep_and_throttle() -> None: if not IS_WINDOWS: return try: kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32 kernel32.SetThreadExecutionState(0x80000000 | 0x00000001 | "बयासी", 83: "तिरासी", 84: "चौरासी", 85: "पचासी", 86: "छियासी", 87: "सत्तासी", 88: "अठासी", 89: "नवासी", 90: "नब्बे", 91: "इक्यानवे", 92: "बानवे", 93: "तिरानवे", 94: "चौरानवे", 95: "पंचानवे", 96: "छियानवे", 97: "सत्तानवे", 98: "अट्ठानवे", 99: "निन्यानवे", 100: "एक सौ", } HINDI_HUNDREDS = { 1: "एक सौ", 2: "दो सौ", 3: "तीन सौ", 4: "चार सौ", 5: "पांच सौ", 6: "छह सौ", 7: "सात सौ", 8: "आठ सौ", 9: "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= 999: hundreds, rem = divmod(n, 100) head = HINDI_HUNDREDS.get(hundreds, f"{hundreds} सौ") return head if rem == 0 else f"{head} {NUM_TO_HINDI.get(rem, str(rem))}" return str(n) def over_ordinal_hindi(over_num: int) -> str: return HINDI_OVER_WORDS.get(over_num, f"{number_to_hindi_words(over_num)}वें") def format_ball_phrase_hindi(ball_str: str) -> str: try: parts = ball_str.strip().split(".") over_num = int(parts[0]) + 1 raw_ball_idx = int(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else 1 ball_idx = min(max(raw_ball_idx, 1), 6) ball_name = HINDI_BALL_WORDS.get(ball_idx, f"{ball_idx}वीं गेंद") return f"{over_ordinal_hindi(over_num)} ओवर की {ball_name}" except Exception: return "अगली गेंद" # ============================================================ # DYNAMIC "ethan": "ईथन", "carl": "कार्ल", "dsouza": "डिसूज़ा", "vicky": "विक्की", "ranjan": "रंजन", "wahab": "वहाब", "hassan": "हसन", "zadran": "जादरान", "panoly": "पनोली", "sabnani": "सबनानी", "parashar": "पाराशर", "asif": "आसिफ", "taniwal": "तानीवाल", "noorullah": "नूरुल्लाह", "ayobi": "अय्यूबी", } def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: """Converts English player names into natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def dismissal_text: str, batter_name: str, batter_runs: str, batter_balls: str ) -> str: batter_hi = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batter_name) runs_val = int(batter_runs) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() else 0 runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_val) balls_hi = ( number_to_hindi_words(batter_balls) if str(batter_balls).isdigit() else "" ) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val > 0: score_status = ( f"{balls_hi} गेंदों में {runs_hi} रन बनाकर" if balls_hi else f"{runs_hi} रन बनाकर" ) elif str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val == 0: score_status = "बिना खाता खोले" else: score_status = "पवेलियन की ओर रुख करते हुए" d_clean = (dismissal_text or "").lower() cb_match = re.search(r"c\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+b\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if cb_match: fielder = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(1).strip()) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(2).strip()) return ( f"विकेट पतन! बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status}, {bowler} की गेंद" f" पर {fielder} को आसान कैच थमा बैठे!" ) b_match = re.search(r"\bb\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if b_match and "c " not in d_clean: bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(b_match.group(1).strip()) return ( {batter_hi} {score_status}" " वापस लौटते हुए!" ) if "run out" in d_clean: return ( f"खराब तालमेल! {batter_hi} {score_status} रन आउट होकर पवेलियन लौटते" " हुए!" ) return ( f"और ये बहुत बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status} आउट होकर पवेलियन" " लौटते हुए!" ) def generate_circle_ball_commentary( ball_num: str, circle_val: Optional[str], bowler_raw: Optional[str], batsman_raw: Optional[str], part.lower() in card_b.lower() for part in batsman_raw.split() if len(part) > 2 ): batter_to_announce = card_b runs_to_announce = last_dismissal.get("runs", "") balls_to_announce = f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ फैलाए, वाइड बॉल!", 2, ) if badge in ("nb", "no से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = अब बड़े शॉट्स की" " तलाश होगी।" ) return ( f"ओवर समाप्त! {over_word} ओवर के बाद कुल स्कोर {runs_hi} रन, {wkts_hi}" f" विकेट के नुकसान पर। {analysis}" ) # ============================================================ # BULLETPROOF SHORT-PATH WINDOWS MCI AUDIO ENGINE # ============================================================ def get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = get_short_path(long_name, output_buf, output_buf_size) return output_buf.value if r > 0 else long_name def play_mp3_native_windows(mp3_path: str): winmm = ctypes.windll.winmm alias = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW("close all", None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type mpegvideo alias {alias}' ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(open_cmd, None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(f"open {safe_path} alias {alias}", None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: return winmm.mciSendStringW(f"play {alias} wait", None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f"close {alias}", None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = ( re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # ============================================================ # NETWORK RESOURCE FILTER # ============================================================ async def route_filter(route: Route) -> None: req = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await route.abort() return await route.continue_() # ============================================================ # MAIN COMMENTARY LOOP # ============================================================ def "नौ सौ", } def number_to_hindi_words(num_str) -> str: try: n = int(str(num_str).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return str(num_str) if n in NUM_TO_HINDI: return NUM_TO_HINDI[n] if 101 <= n <= natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def Array.from(collected); }""") count = 0 for name in names: for part in name.split(): p_clean = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", part).lower() if p_clean and len(p_clean) > 2 and p_clean not in से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW("close all", None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type winmm.mciSendStringW(f"play {alias} wait", None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f"close {alias}", None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = ( re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # Here is the clean, de-duplicated, and fully verified production script: Python #!/usr/bin/env python3 """PREET SPORTS - Professional Cricket Radio Engine (Accurate Wicket & Batsman Edition) natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. से दौड़ते हुए", ) def build_live_wicket_speech( dismissal_text: str, batter_name: str, batter_runs: str, batter_balls: str ) -> str: batter_hi = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batter_name) runs_val = int(batter_runs) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() else 0 runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_val) balls_hi = ( number_to_hindi_words(batter_balls) if str(batter_balls).isdigit() else "" ) if str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val > 0: score_status = ( f"{balls_hi} गेंदों में {runs_hi} रन बनाकर" if balls_hi else f"{runs_hi} रन बनाकर" ) elif str(batter_runs).isdigit() and runs_val == 0: score_status = "बिना खाता खोले" else: score_status = "पवेलियन की ओर रुख करते हुए" d_clean = (dismissal_text or "").lower() cb_match = re.search(r"c\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+b\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if cb_match: fielder = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(1).strip()) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(cb_match.group(2).strip()) return ( f"विकेट पतन! बड़ा झटका! {batter_hi} {score_status}, {bowler} की गेंद" f" पर {fielder} को आसान कैच थमा बैठे!" ) b_match = re.search(r"\bb\s+([A-Za-z\s]+)", d_clean) if b_match and "c " not in d_clean: bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(b_match.group(1).strip()) return ( f"गिल्लियां हवा में बिखर गईं! क्लीन बोल्ड! {batter_hi} {score_status}," f" {bowler} की जादुई गेंद पर बोल्ड हो गए!" ) if "lbw" in d_clean: return ( f"अंपायर की उंगली हवा में! एलबीडब्ल्यू आउट! {batter_hi} {score_status}" " वापस लौटते हुए!" ) if "run out" in d_clean: return ( f"खराब तालमेल! {batter_hi} {score_status} रन आउट होकर पवेलियन last_dismissal.get("batter", "") if batsman_raw and any( part.lower() in card_b.lower() for part in batsman_raw.split() if len(part) > 2 ): batter_to_announce = card_b runs_to_announce = runs_to_announce, balls_to_announce ) return f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ random.choice(HINGLISH_SIX) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "4": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_FOUR) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "3": action = ( "शानदार रनिंग बिटवीन द विकेट्स, दोनों बल्लेबाजों ने action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOT) rate_level = 1 runup = random.choice(BOWLER_RUNUPS) if bowler and batsman: text = ( f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} {runup} गेंद डालते हुए {batsman} को," f" {action}" ) elif bowler: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} गेंदबाजी छोर से तैयार, {action}" else: text = f"{ball_phrase}, {action}" return text, rate_level def build_over_summary(over_int: int, runs, wkts) -> str: over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = int(str(runs).strip()) wkts_num = int(str(wkts).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return f"और इस तरह {over_word} ओवर का खेल समाप्त हुआ।" runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_num) wkts_hi = number_to_hindi_words(wkts_num) crr = round(runs_num / max(over_int, 1), 2) crr_hi = number_to_hindi_words(int(crr)) if crr >= 8.5: analysis = ( f"करंट रन रेट {crr_hi} के पार पहुंच चुका है, बल्लेबाज पूरी तरह हावी" " हैं।" ) elif crr >= 6.5: analysis = ( f"रन गति लगभग {crr_hi} प्रति ओवर की है, दोनों टीमें रणनीति पर कायम" " हैं।" ) else: analysis = ( f"रन गति {crr_hi} के आसपास थोड़ी धीमी जरूर है, अब बड़े शॉट्स की" " तलाश होगी।" ) return ( f"ओवर समाप्त! {over_word} ओवर के बाद कुल स्कोर {runs_hi} रन, {wkts_hi}" f" विकेट के नुकसान पर। {analysis}" ) # ============================================================ # BULLETPROOF SHORT-PATH WINDOWS MCI AUDIO ENGINE # ============================================================ def get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = get_short_path(long_name, output_buf, output_buf_size) return output_buf.value if r > 0 else long_name def play_mp3_native_windows(mp3_path: str): winmm = ctypes.windll.winmm alias = f"track_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time() * 1000) % 10000}" safe_path = get_short_path_name(os.path.abspath(mp3_path)) winmm.mciSendStringW("close all", None, 0, 0) open_cmd = f'open "{safe_path}" type mpegvideo alias {alias}' ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(open_cmd, None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: ret = winmm.mciSendStringW(f"open {safe_path} alias {alias}", None, 0, 0) if ret != 0: return winmm.mciSendStringW(f"play {alias} wait", None, 0, 0) winmm.mciSendStringW(f"close {alias}", None, 0, 0) async def generate_and_play_audio(text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: clean_text = ( re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, play_mp3_native_windows, temp_mp3) except Exception as e: log.error("TTS Audio Error: %s", e) finally: if os.path.exists(temp_mp3): try: os.remove(temp_mp3) except OSError: pass class RadioAnnouncer: def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ @dataclass(frozen=True) class BallSnapshot: ball: Optional[str] circle_val: Optional[str] bowler: str batsman: str runs: Optional[int] wkts: Optional[int] is_over_complete: bool = False is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # ============================================================ # NETWORK RESOURCE FILTER # ============================================================ async def route_filter(route: Route) -> None: req = route.request if req.resource_type in BLOCKED_RESOURCE_TYPES: await route.abort() return url_lower = req.url.lower() if any(pattern in url_lower for pattern in BLOCKED_URL_PATTERNS): await route.abort() return await route.continue_() # ============================================================ # MAIN COMMENTARY LOOP # ============================================================ def print_status(message: str) -> None: print(f"\r{message:<70}", end="", flush=True) async def open_match_page(page: Page, target_url: str) -> None: try: await page.goto( target_url, "ethan": "ईथन", "carl": "कार्ल", "dsouza": "डिसूज़ा", "vicky": "विक्की", "ranjan": "रंजन", "wahab": "वहाब", "hassan": "हसन", "zadran": "जादरान", "panoly": "पनोली", "sabnani": "सबनानी", "parashar": "पाराशर", "asif": "आसिफ", "taniwal": "तानीवाल", "noorullah": "नूरुल्लाह", "ayobi": "अय्यूबी", } def clean_player_name_to_hindi(raw_name: Optional[str]) -> str: """Converts English player names into natural Devanagari Hindi phonetics to eliminate slow or stretched vowels in Edge-TTS. """ if not raw_name: return "" cleaned = re.sub( r"\b(to|wd|nb|wide|no\s*runs?|runs?|dots?|out|c|b|wkt|wk|\d+)\b", "", raw_name, flags=re.IGNORECASE, ) cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z\s]", " ", cleaned).strip() words = cleaned.split() if not words: return "" result = [] for word in words: lower_w = word.lower() # 1. Match from live scraped squad cache if lower_w in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: result.append(DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[lower_w]) continue # 2. Match from pre-mapped core phonetic dict if lower_w in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: result.append(CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[lower_w]) continue # 3. Handle Indian names ending in 'av' or 'ar' that get stretched if lower_w.endswith("av"): # e.g., Yadav -> Yuduv sound trimmed = lower_w[:-2] res = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT.get(trimmed, trimmed) + "व" result.append(res) elif lower_w.endswith("an") and len(lower_w) > 4: result.append(lower_w.capitalize()) else: result.append(word.capitalize()) return " ".join(result) async def Array.from(collected); }""") count = 0 for name in names: for part in name.split(): p_clean = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z]", "", part).lower() if p_clean and len(p_clean) > 2 and p_clean not in DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE: if p_clean in CORE_PHONETIC_DICT: DYNAMIC_PLAYER_CACHE[p_clean] = CORE_PHONETIC_DICT[p_clean] count += 1 print( f"📋 Auto-loaded {len(names)} players from CREX DOM ({count} get_short_path_name(long_name: str) -> str: output_buf_size = 260 output_buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(output_buf_size) get_short_path = ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetShortPathNameW r = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", text) .replace("\r", " ") .replace("\n", " ") .strip() ) # Slightly boosted tempo clips vowels naturally and prevents dragging if rate_level >= 3: rate_str = "+18%" pitch_str = "+4Hz" elif rate_level == 2: rate_str = "+10%" pitch_str = "+1Hz" else: rate_str = "+5%" pitch_str = "+0Hz" temp_mp3 = os.path.join( tempfile.gettempdir(), f"_radio_{os.getpid()}_{time.time_ns()}.mp3" ) try: comm = edge_tts.Communicate( clean_text, VOICE_NAME, rate=rate_str, pitch=pitch_str ) await comm.save(temp_mp3) loop = asyncio.get_running_loop() await loop.run_in_executor(None, def __init__(self) -> None: self._queue: asyncio.Queue[tuple[str, int]] = asyncio.Queue() self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None def start(self) -> None: self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._worker(), name="radio_announcer") async def say(self, text: str, rate_level: int = 2) -> None: await self._queue.put((text, rate_level)) async def stop(self) -> None: if self._task: self._task.cancel() try: await self._task except asyncio.CancelledError: pass self._task = None async def _worker(self) -> None: while True: text, rate_level = await self._queue.get() try: print(f"\n🎙️ [MADHUR RADIO PRO | LEVEL {rate_level}]: {text}") await generate_and_play_audio(text, rate_level) except asyncio.CancelledError: raise except Exception: pass finally: self._queue.task_done() # ============================================================ # LIVE MATCH & DOM EXTRACTOR # ============================================================ is_innings_break: bool = False last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None @property def has_ball(self) -> bool: return bool(self.ball and self.circle_val) async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # def announce_over_end( page: Page, announcer: RadioAnnouncer, ball_num: str ) -> None: await asyncio.sleep(OVER_SETTLE_SECONDS) fresh = await extract_circle_balls_data(page) runs = fresh.runs if fresh bowler_raw: Optional[str], batsman_raw: Optional[str], last_dismissal: Optional[dict] = None, ) -> tuple[str, int]: ball_phrase = format_ball_phrase_hindi(ball_num) bowler = clean_player_name_to_hindi(bowler_raw) batsman = clean_player_name_to_hindi(batsman_raw) badge = (circle_val or "").strip().lower() # WICKET PRIORITY if badge in ("w", "wkt", "out"): last_dismissal.get("batter", "") if batsman_raw and any( part.lower() in card_b.lower() for part in batsman_raw.split() if len(part) > 2 ): batter_to_announce = card_b runs_to_announce = runs_to_announce, balls_to_announce ) return f"{ball_phrase}, {wkt_speech}", 3 if badge in ("wd", "wide"): return ( f"{ball_phrase}, दिशाहीन गेंदबाजी, लेग स्टंप से काफी बाहर, अंपायर ने" " दोनों हाथ random.choice(HINGLISH_SIX) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "4": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_FOUR) rate_level = 3 elif badge == "3": action = ( "शानदार रनिंग बिटवीन द विकेट्स, दोनों बल्लेबाजों ने दौड़कर तीन रन" " पूरे किए!" ) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "2": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOUBLE) rate_level = 2 elif badge == "1": action = random.choice(HINGLISH_SINGLE) rate_level = 2 else: action = random.choice(HINGLISH_DOT) rate_level = 1 runup = random.choice(BOWLER_RUNUPS) if bowler and batsman: text = ( f"{ball_phrase}, {bowler} {runup} गेंद डालते हुए {batsman} को," f" {action}" ) over_word = over_ordinal_hindi(over_int) try: runs_num = int(str(runs).strip()) wkts_num = int(str(wkts).strip()) except (TypeError, ValueError): return f"और इस तरह {over_word} ओवर का खेल समाप्त हुआ।" runs_hi = number_to_hindi_words(runs_num) wkts_hi = number_to_hindi_words(wkts_num) crr = round(runs_num / max(over_int, 1), 2) crr_hi = number_to_hindi_words(int(crr)) if crr >= 8.5: analysis = ( f"करंट रन रेट {crr_hi} के पार पहुंच चुका है, बल्लेबाज पूरी तरह हावी" " हैं।" ) elif crr >= 6.5: analysis = ( f"रन गति लगभग {crr_hi} प्रति ओवर की है, दोनों टीमें रणनीति पर कायम" " हैं।" ) else: analysis = ( f"रन गति {crr_hi} के आसपास थोड़ी धीमी जरूर है, अब बड़े शॉट्स की" " तलाश होगी।" ) return ( f"ओवर समाप्त! {over_word} ओवर के बाद कुल स्कोर {runs_hi} रन, {wkts_hi}" f" विकेट के नुकसान पर। {analysis}" ) # ============================================================ # BULLETPROOF SHORT-PATH WINDOWS MCI AUDIO ENGINE # ============================================================ def async def extract_circle_balls_data(page: Page) -> Optional[BallSnapshot]: try: raw = await page.evaluate(r"""() => { let ballNum = null; let circleVal = null; let bowler = ''; let batsman = ''; let runs = null; let wkts = null; let isOverComplete = false; let isInningsBreak = false; let lastDismissal = null; const bodyText = document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''; const title = document.title || ''; if (/\((?:10\.0\Vert{}20\.0\Vert{}50\.0)\)/.test(title) || /Innings Break/i.test(bodyText) || /-[1][0]\b/.test(title)) { isInningsBreak = true; } // 1. SCAN ONLY THE ACTIVE OVER STRIP let latestOverNum = null; let activeBalls = []; const overRows = document.querySelectorAll( 'div[class*="over-card"], div[class*="recent-over"], div[class*="overRow"], div[class*="overWrap"], div[class*="over"]' ); for (let row of overRows) { const rowText = row.innerText || ''; const m = rowText.match(/\bOver\s+(\d+)\b/i); if (m) { const parsedOver = parseInt(m[1], 10); const candidateNodes = row.querySelectorAll('span, b, div[class*="ball"], div[class*="circle"]'); let rowBalls = []; for (let node of candidateNodes) { if (node.children.length === 0) { const val = (node.innerText || '').trim(); if (/^(0|1|2|3|4|6|w|wkt|wd|nb|wide)$/i.test(val)) { rowBalls.push(val); } } } if (rowBalls.length > 0 && rowBalls.length <= 12) { latestOverNum = parsedOver - 1; activeBalls = rowBalls; } } } if (latestOverNum !== null && activeBalls.length > 0) { let legalDeliveries = 0; for (let v of activeBalls) { if (!/^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(v)) legalDeliveries++; } const clampedBallIndex = Math.min(Math.max(legalDeliveries, 1), 6); ballNum = `${latestOverNum}.${clampedBallIndex}`; circleVal = activeBalls[activeBalls.length - 1]; const isExtra = /^(wd|wide|nb)$/i.test(circleVal.toLowerCase()); if (legalDeliveries === 6 && !isExtra) { isOverComplete = true; } } if (!ballNum) { const titleOversMatch = title.match(/\((\d{1,2}\.([1-6]))\)/); if (titleOversMatch) ballNum = titleOversMatch[1]; } // 2. PARSE EXACT DELIVERY ROW FOR STRIKER & BOWLER const commItems = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="item"], div[class*="update"], div[class*="comm-card"], div[class*="row"]'); for (let el of commItems) { const text = el.innerText || ''; const matchTo = text.match(/([A-Za-z\s]+?)\s+to\s+([A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:,|\n|$)/i); if (matchTo) { bowler = matchTo[1].trim(); batsman = matchTo[2].trim(); if (!circleVal) { const badgeEl = el.querySelector('span[class*="run"], span[class*="badge"], div[class*="run"]'); if (badgeEl) circleVal = badgeEl.innerText.trim(); } break; } } // 3. TARGET THE LIVE RED WICKET DISMISSAL CARD const allDivs = document.querySelectorAll('div'); for (let d of allDivs) { const txt = d.innerText || ''; if (txt.includes('Wkt #') || (txt.includes('OUT') && (txt.includes(' b ') || txt.includes('c ')))) { const cardMatch = txt.match(/([A-Za-z\s]{2,25})\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*\)/); if (cardMatch) { const rawName = cardMatch[1].replace(/OUT|Wkt\s*#\d+/gi, '').trim(); const bRuns = cardMatch[2].trim(); const bBalls = cardMatch[3].trim(); let howOut = ''; const howM = txt.match(/(c\s+[A-Za-z\s]+?\s+b\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|\bb\s+[A-Za-z\s]+|lbw|run\s*out)/i); if (howM) howOut = howM[1].trim(); lastDismissal = { batter: rawName, runs: bRuns, balls: bBalls, howOut: howOut }; break; } } } // 4. Exact Live Score const titleScoreMatch = title.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\s*\(/); if (titleScoreMatch) { runs = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(titleScoreMatch[3], 10); } if (runs === null) { const topScoreCards = document.querySelectorAll('div[class*="team-score"], div[class*="score-card"], div[class*="match-header"]'); for (let c of topScoreCards) { const t = c.innerText || ''; const m = t.match(/([A-Za-z0-9\-]+)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*[-/]\s*([0-9]|10)\b/); if (m) { runs = parseInt(m[2], 10); wkts = parseInt(m[3], 10); break; } } } return { ball: ballNum, circleVal: circleVal, bowler: bowler, batsman: batsman, runs: runs, wkts: wkts, isOverComplete: isOverComplete, isInningsBreak: isInningsBreak, lastDismissal: lastDismissal }; }""") if not raw: return None return BallSnapshot( ball=raw.get("ball"), circle_val=raw.get("circleVal"), bowler=raw.get("bowler") or "", batsman=raw.get("batsman") or "", runs=raw.get("runs"), wkts=raw.get("wkts"), is_over_complete=raw.get("isOverComplete", False), is_innings_break=raw.get("isInningsBreak", False), last_dismissal=raw.get("lastDismissal"), ) except Exception: return None # The layout, neon styling, and visual structure look clean and professional, but there are critical data sync and display bugs visible on screen that cause cricket fans to immediately realize something is off and bounce: 1. The Flag/Team Misalignment Bug (Biggest Issue) On the left, the team label says IND, but the circular badge is displaying the West Indies cricket logo. JPG On the right, the team label says WI, and it also displays the West Indies cricket logo. JPG Below that, the toss text says "IND opt to Bowl", but India is shown batting at 176-3 while West Indies is at 0-0. In actual fact (as seen in your browser tab title "WI 176-3"), it is West Indies batting. JPG + 1 Why it matters: Cricket fans notice swapped logos and incorrect batting teams within 3 seconds. When they see the wrong team batting with duplicate flags, they assume the scorecard is broken and close the stream. 2. Browser Admin Bar Visible in OBS The top yellow admin controls (CREX MATCH: ..., LOAD MATCH, COPY OBS URL, LIVE • SYNCED) are rendering directly on the screen. JPG Fix in CSS: Add a dedicated display mode or URL parameter (e.g. ?overlay=true or #obs) that sets the top setup bar to display: none; so viewers only see the actual broadcast scoreboard: CSS /* Hide admin controls when viewed in OBS */ .admin-bar, .crex-controls { display: none !important; } 3. Missing Active Match Context in the Center The large stadium background takes up roughly 50% of the entire middle screen space, but it is completely empty and static. JPG On mobile screens, this large dark empty space looks frozen. Use this center area for dynamic retention elements: Current Over / Ball-by-Ball Live Tracker (moved down or enlarged). Live Animated Win Predictor % (e.g. IND 42% | WI 58%). Active Commentary Ticker: Display the latest 1–2 lines of Hindi commentary or ball status right across the middle. 4. Player Portrait & Name Sync The bowler card shows P Yadav (1-25) and batsmen S Hope 76(40) and S Rutherford 7(4). JPG If your Python radio engine is announcing the balls while this board is running on Render, make sure the ball event triggers a brief pulse / highlight animation on the batsman facing the delivery and the active bowler. A tiny animated indicator on the cricket ball next to the bowler signals to the viewer that the stream is 100% real-time and not frozen.
